## 11. Contradictions with ARCH Rev 9.2

**None open against the frozen text; r14 files one errata request (§ERRATA-REQUEST).** C-11 to C-17 and D-HALT are resolved by Rev 9.2 and deleted (§R4). The observations below stay for the owners named. O-3 is withdrawn in r14, and O-8 and O-9 are new:

- **O-1 (ARCH-0 owner of `test_no_unit_overlaps_launch_window`).** `breezy-quote-tape-ingest-frequent.timer` fires every 15 min, so at 16:30Z, 16:45Z and 17:00Z, and is not a row of ARCH's §5.2 launch-window table. AUT-6 adds no exemption for it (r3's blanket data-path exemption is withdrawn); the test author lists it as a launch-path row or reschedules it.
- **O-2 (AUT-5 and the memory test).** ARCH §5.2 compares "the sum of effective limits plus measured node and recorder RSS" with `MemAvailable` without saying whether the node is running when `MemAvailable` is read. AUT-6 adds back the memory already resident in the counted processes so nothing is counted twice (§3.10.1 item 6). This is a reading, not an amendment.
- **O-3 (r7, LOW-4): withdrawn in r14.** The drill probe, the HUNG class and `SELF_HEAL_RESTARTABLE_UNITS` are deleted, so the reading of HUNG for a drill target has no subject left. r13 §11 keeps the text.
- **O-4 (r7, U4; ARCH §5.2 Memory owner).** ARCH §5.2 says the quote-tape-ingest drop-in stands "until ING-2 S3a". No item of that name exists; per the coordinator's correction AUT-6 reads it as the ING-2-AMEND2 (`docs/core/PROGRESS.md:52`) drop-in removal and cites it by that name everywhere. This is a label reading; the frozen text is not edited and the rule (the sum FAILs by name while the drop-in stands) is unchanged.
- **O-5 (r8, E-7; AUT-5, owner of the bwrap wrapper and self-probe).** E-7 binds the demand producer to `registry/demand/` and `evidence/alerts` only, while ARCH C5 has the producer write its demand file "beside its verdict" under `derived/verdicts/`, which that bind set excludes. AUT-6 reconciles the two with two stages of one `aut6.intraday` invocation. The evaluate stage (unwrapped in r8–r10; from r11 in its own bwrap row binding `derived/verdicts`, §3.1.1) writes the verdicts; the wrapped demand stage writes only the demand files, in the same invocation and seconds later (§3.4.2). The demand bind's positive control uses `O_WRONLY|O_TMPFILE` rather than `O_CREAT`, so that no named file ever appears where the watch actor treats an unexpected name as a venue veto. The negative control and the alerts-bind control use `O_WRONLY|O_CREAT` exactly as E-7 states. **(r11, E-7a)** The same reading covers the `derived/verdicts/` positive control of the evaluate, daily and health rows, so no probe name appears where a C4 listing could see it. This is a reading, not an amendment.
- **O-6 (r11, E-7a; AUT-5, owner of the wrapper, the table and the snapshot helper).** Two readings, none an amendment. r11's (ii) is withdrawn in r12, because erratum E-8a now states the rule: node-up readers pass `take_flock=False` and are advisory (§3.1.1).
  - **(i) Row key.** E-7a keys rows by unit name, but the intraday unit's two stages need different binds. AUT-6 therefore keys them `<unit>#<stage>`, and the wrapper still fails closed on any unknown key.
  - **(iii) Import closure.** E-7a rule 2 implies the venue-adapter import-closure rule for every wrapped unit. AUT-6 meets it as written, never by an exemption and never by narrowing the rule (the AF1 ruling rejects option (b)). It moves pure code to adapter-free modules, one implementation each: `leg_prices` (r11), and in r12 the halt decode, the lock-holder parser, `quantile_density` and `location_correction`, and the calibration leg (§3.1.2). The result is measured: 0 adapter modules in the evaluate and daily closures (§0 (21)).
- **O-7 (r12, AF1; the ARCH-0 layer owner and AUT-4).** Two readings, none an amendment.
  - **(a) Domain I/O.** `src/breezy/domain/family_halt.py` is the first `breezy.domain` module that performs I/O: `read_family_halt_rows_readonly` opens a read-only SQLite URI. The AF1 ruling names a domain module, and import-linter's layers contract constrains only `breezy.*` imports, so this changes no contract. If the layer owner prefers `domain` to stay I/O-free, the reader alone can move one layer up, to `breezy.persistence`, with the same alias. That would change neither closure.
  - **(b) AUT-4's replay closure.** `run_live_parity`'s closure holds 33 adapter modules, because `scripts/analysis/nbp_shadow_parity.py` imports `breezy.adapters.polymarket_us.symbology` directly (§0 (20)). AUT-4 moves it to `src/breezy/analysis/autonomy/shadow_replay.py`, so any autonomy unit that runs it under E-7a meets the same closure rule. That is AUT-4's to resolve. AUT-6 only consumes AUT-4's C4 `eval_replay_path` verdict (r13, AG1; §3.4.4).
- **O-8 (r14; the coordinator and AUT-1, owner of the recorder unit): the recorder's start limit.** This is a stated deviation from ruling item 1's "`StartLimitBurst`/`StartLimitIntervalSec` for the cap".
  - **Evidence.**
    - The recorder sets `StartLimitIntervalSec=0`, citing the 2026-09-09 incident: start-limit exhaustion parked capture for 6.7 h of tape that cannot be re-fetched (§0.1 (23)).
    - systemd counts every start against the limit, crash restarts included (§0.1 (24), (28)).
    - So any finite limit on the recorder re-opens that incident.
  - **AUT-6's default.** Keep `0`. Bound the restarts by the existing backoff (30 s → 480 s) and page every watchdog restart (AUT-1's stop hook, with #21 as the fallback).
  - **Other members.** A member that already declares a finite start limit keeps it, and `OnFailure=` pages its exhaustion.
  - **If overruled.** WP4 adds `StartLimitBurst=3`/`StartLimitIntervalSec=86400` (R-43).
  - ER-1's replacement text covers both outcomes.
- **X-1 to X-3 (r14; cross-plan with AUT-1 r9, which was drafted at the same time).** AUT-1 r9 owns the recorder's gate, sender, unit lines and stop hook (§3.5). Its module map differs from AUT-6's SELF_HEAL contract in three places. These are amendment requests for AUT-1's brief, not AUT-6 design gaps, and AUT-6's config and contract tests fail until they are met:
  - **X-1.** `NotifyAccess=main` → `all` (ruling item 5; measured in §0.1 (27)).
  - **X-2.** Add `Type=notify`, with `READY=1` on the first allowed ping (ruling item 1).
  - **X-3.** Put the launch-window deferral in `classify_recorder_sample` (ruling item 4, ARCH V7).

  AUT-1 r9's `WatchdogSec=600`, `WatchdogSignal=SIGTERM`, `Restart=always` and its stop hook already meet the contract.
- **O-9 (r14; the README live-proof rule): the SELF_HEAL live proof.**
  - **What changed.** The ruling deletes the drill probe and proves the mechanism once, with a scratch transient unit, at WP verify-first.
  - **Reading.** The live path for SELF_HEAL is systemd itself. A real recorder watchdog restart in the window is therefore the natural proof. If none occurs, the WP4 V-W1 record is the injected proof: it runs the production gate under the deployed directives, on a scratch unit, never on the recorder, because an injected stall would lose tape. `test_systemd_restarts_silent_notifier_on_watchdog` must also be green at the window sha.
  - **Status.** This is a reading for the scorer, not an amendment.

## §ERRATA-REQUEST (r14): ARCH Rev 9.2 SELF_HEAL-executor wording

This request goes to the coordinator, the owner of `reviews/ARCH-ERRATA-rev9_2.md`. The frozen ARCH text is not edited. AUT-6 asks for one erratum, proposed as **E-11**, with the replacement text below. Under it, SELF_HEAL is realised by systemd's `Restart=` on watchdog, and AUT-6 counts the restarts and pages them (ruling item 6). Each item quotes the current text of `reviews/snapshots/ARCH_rev9_2.md` (sha256 `1b288d0e0172b233b791d875845e8002824a52d4a2e7819685ec0f2057572f42`) exactly, with line numbers, then gives the exact replacement. The §R9 disposition rows V8 (line 1277) and U11 (line 1302) are history and are not edited. The C4 `declared_action_class` enum keeps `SELF_HEAL`.

**ER-1. §4.5 ceilings table, lines 938–939.** Current:

```
| `SELF_HEAL_RESTARTABLE_UNITS` | literal tuple of unit names; never the supervisor, the trade node or the engine; argv `try-restart --no-block` only, with a `subprocess` timeout ≤ 30 s (G40, P1-10, V8), no unit-file or env edits; `try-restart` of an **active** allowlisted recorder classified HUNG is permitted (U11) |
| `SELF_HEAL_MAX_RESTARTS_PER_UNIT_PER_DAY` (Z15, W11) | ≤ 3, then alert only; counted per **trading day** [16:45Z, next 16:45Z), so an overnight exhaustion resets before LAUNCH (P1-12), from write-once records in `evidence/selfheal/<trading_date>/` (the UTC date of the window's 16:45Z start) |
```

Replacement:

```
| `WATCHDOG_DAEMON_UNITS` (SELF_HEAL membership; E-11) | literal tuple of unit names; never the supervisor, the trade node, the engine or the dead-man; each member is `Type=notify`, `NotifyAccess=all`, `WatchdogSec` in [120 s, 600 s], `Restart=` a value that restarts on a watchdog timeout, `WatchdogSignal=SIGTERM` or `SIGABRT` with `LimitCORE=0`; the member sends `WATCHDOG=1` only while its capture counters advance; no autonomy module issues a `systemctl` restart, start or stop (G40, P1-10, V8, U11) |
| SELF_HEAL cap (Z15, W11; E-11) | the member's own `StartLimitBurst` ≤ 3 per `StartLimitIntervalSec`, with `OnFailure=` the delivery-proven notifier, which pages exhaustion CRITICAL; a member whose unit file disables the start limit for a recorded incident (the recorder, `StartLimitIntervalSec=0`, 2026-09-09) is bounded by its restart backoff (`RestartSteps`/`RestartMaxDelaySec`) instead, and every watchdog restart is paged CRITICAL with delivery proof by the member's stop hook, with a journal-based fallback (P1-12) |
```

**ER-2. §4.5 tests and the self-heal counter, lines 949–955.** Current, from `` `test_self_heal_unit_allowlist_is_literal_and_excludes_trade` `` to the end of the paragraph:

```
`test_self_heal_unit_allowlist_is_literal_and_excludes_trade`
(AST: the restart call site takes only members of the tuple, as an argv list
`["systemctl", "--user", "try-restart", "--no-block", unit]` with a `subprocess` timeout, never `shell=True`, within the per-unit daily count; the tuple contains
no `breezy-trade*` or `breezy-autonomy-engine*` name; no autonomy module writes under `~/.config/systemd` or
`~/.config/breezy`). **Self-heal counter (W11, L-50):** one write-once record per restart,
`evidence/selfheal/<trading_date>/<ts_ns>_<unit>.json`, counted before every restart; an unreadable directory means no
restart and an alert (`test_self_heal_cap_survives_process_restart`, `test_restart_window_resets_before_launch`).
```

Replacement:

```
`test_watchdog_units_are_literal_and_exclude_trade_supervisor_engine`
(the deploy units declaring `WatchdogSec` equal the literal tuple, which contains no `breezy-trade*`, supervisor,
`breezy-autonomy-engine*` or dead-man name; no autonomy module calls `systemctl` with `restart`, `try-restart`, `start`
or `stop`; no autonomy module writes under `~/.config/systemd` or `~/.config/breezy`). **Self-heal cap (W11, L-50;
E-11):** held by systemd (`StartLimitBurst`/`StartLimitIntervalSec`, or the recorded backoff exception above), which
survives any autonomy process restart by construction; no record file exists. Tests:
`test_watchdog_unit_config_declares_notify_watchdog_restart_startlimit_and_notifyaccess_all` (replaces
`test_self_heal_cap_survives_process_restart`) and `test_watchdog_restart_paged_once_via_stop_hook_record_else_fallback_critical`
(replaces `test_restart_window_resets_before_launch`).
```

**ER-3. §4.6 Action executors (P6-7), lines 964–968.** Current:

```
- **Action executors (P6-7).** ENTRY_VETO: `RegistryWatchActor` (AUT-5). DEMOTE, HALT: the intraday engine pass (AUT-5).
  SELF_HEAL: AUT-6's `breezy-autonomy-health` oneshot (a C4 producer pinned as `aut6.health`, P6-13), the **only**
```

```
  SELF_HEAL (E-11): systemd, through each `WATCHDOG_DAEMON_UNITS` member's `Type=notify` + `WatchdogSec=` + `Restart=`
  (on watchdog) + `StartLimit*` + `OnFailure=` directives; the member sends `WATCHDOG=1` only while its capture counters
  advance (AUT-1 owns the gate, its sender, its call site, the unit lines and a stop hook that pages each kill; AUT-6
  owns the SELF_HEAL contract they must meet). AUT-6's `breezy-autonomy-health` oneshot (a C4 producer pinned as

  HEALTH verdict and pages as the fallback to the stop hook; it makes no state-changing call. ALERT: the
  producing unit, through `deliver_with_proof` (CRITICAL) or `emit_alert`. SELF_HEAL needs no policy ruling to arm; its
  live clocks start at activation.
```

### R14-D. Native-path tests added (all in WP4)

- **The unit-file config test, bwrap included.** `test_watchdog_unit_config_declares_notify_watchdog_restart_startlimit_and_notifyaccess_all` covers `WatchdogSec`, `Restart` (on watchdog), `StartLimit*`, `NotifyAccess=all` (plain, `timeout`-prefixed and bwrap-wrapped shapes), the `WatchdogSignal`/`LimitCORE` rule, and `OnFailure=` whenever a start limit is finite. Companions: `test_every_unit_with_watchdogsec_is_a_listed_member`, `test_watchdog_units_are_literal_and_exclude_trade_supervisor_engine` and `test_watchdog_member_execstart_reaches_a_notify_sender`.
- **The ping is withheld when counters stall.** `test_ping_withheld_when_counters_stall`, a contract test on AUT-1's `classify_recorder_sample` in `tests/unit/test_aut6_watchdog_contract.py`, plus that file's empty-listing, launch-window, READY and notify cases.
- **systemd restarts a silent notifier (verify-first scratch transient unit).** `tests/integration/test_aut6_watchdog_scratch_unit.py::test_systemd_restarts_silent_notifier_on_watchdog`, with `test_notifyaccess_main_behind_bwrap_times_out` and `test_onfailure_target_sees_auto_restart_then_failed`. At plan time it ran by hand as §0.1 (24)–(29).
- **Notifier and health pass.** The watchdog branches (`test_watchdog_failure_with_restart_pending_sends_nothing`, `test_watchdog_start_limit_exhausted_pages_critical_via_markers`, `test_unreadable_unit_state_pages`), #21 versus `daemon_crashed`, the de-duplication against `daemon_auto_restarted`, `watchdog_disarmed`, and the `memory_ceiling_suspect` CRITICAL.

### R14-E. L-1 null-hypothesis rows: every custom component that remains

| Remaining custom component | Native capability checked (evidence) | Verdict |
|---|---|---|
| SELF_HEAL contract tests and `WATCHDOG_DAEMON_UNITS` (new; the gate and sender are AUT-1 r9's) | systemd decides and performs the restart (§0.1 (24)). Only the daemon knows whether capture advances, so the gate belongs to the daemon's owner, AUT-1 (`recorder_watchdog.py`). Nautilus 1.231.0 and the venv have no `sd_notify` (§0.1 (23)). | AUT-6 builds only two literals and its tests; no second gate or sender (DRY) |
| Notifier watchdog branch (`study_failure_notifier`) | `OnFailure=` starts a unit but carries no state, and it fires on every watchdog failure, not only at exhaustion (§0.1 (25)). The unit's own `SubState` tells the two cases apart (measured). | Extend the existing notifier with one property read |
| `deliver_with_proof`, per-attempt records | Nautilus has no external alert delivery (`TeeAlertSink`/`emit_alert` swallow failure, `src/breezy/runtime/health.py:479-510`). systemd's `OnFailure=` only starts a unit and proves no HTTP 2xx. | Keep (ruling item 5) |
| Durable outbox, claim and reclaim, redeliver unit | systemd and journald give no at-least-once delivery queue. Nautilus external msgbus streams would need a Redis backend that is not deployed here and that NO-SEND does not cover. `record_skip` retries only its own alert (`scripts/analysis/replay_daily_runner.py:733-762`). | Keep (ruling item 5) |
| Canary and off-host absence | No native off-host heartbeat exists. The absence rule is the receiver's (WP2 verify-first). | Keep (ruling item 5) |
| VERDICT detectors and producers | Nautilus has no drift or calibration detectors. Reused: `FeeDriftProbeActor`, `all_refused_halt_reason`, `order_book_depth10`, `evaluate_calibration_leg` (moved) and AUT-4's `eval_replay_path` verdict. | Keep (ruling item 5) |
| Unit-health journal sweep, classification, commit order, cursor, rollup (narrowed) | `OnFailure=` must be declared per unit. The failures that matter most are agent `run-*` transients, which carry no `OnFailure=` (§3.8: `run-p814078`, `breezy-parity-mem-*`), so a journal sweep is required. `list-units --failed` misses fail-then-succeed between passes (§3.9 G5). systemd has no "explained" ledger. | Keep, narrowed (ruling item 3) |
| Timer liveness #28 | `Persistent=` catches up a missed run but never pages, and nothing native reports a disabled or stalled timer. | Keep |
| Memory check #31 | systemd enforces `MemoryMax` per unit only. Nothing native sums limits across units against `MemAvailable` (ARCH §5.2 V14). | Keep (ruling item 5) |
| Discovery-pull redesign | It fixes an existing script's working set. Raising `MemoryMax` is the rejected alternative (L-49, L-53). | Keep (ruling item 5) |
| bwrap rows and self-probe (E-7a) | systemd user-unit mount sandboxing is not enforced on this host (E-7, measured). | Keep (ruling item 5) |
| Health heartbeat and #26, #27, #29 meta-detectors | AUT-5's dead-man is the native-to-programme watcher. systemd has no cross-unit staleness check. A `WatchdogSec` on a oneshot health unit would not express "last completed pass". | Keep |
| INTEGRITY-floor demand write | ARCH P6-12. It reuses AUT-5's `demand/v1` writer. | Keep |
| Build-side restart marker CLI | The journal records `JOB_TYPE=stop`, but not the intent or the commit (§0, r8 measurements). | Keep (one small CLI) |
| Domain moves (§3.1.2) | These are moves of existing code, with one implementation each, not new components. | n/a |

### R14-F. Verify-first summary and cleanup

- **What ran.** The scratch units `claude-aut6r14-a`…`f` and the linked `claude-aut6r14-onfail{,@}.service` targets ran between 16:10Z and 16:15Z.
- **Cleanup.** They were then stopped, `reset-failed` and unlinked, and `daemon-reload` was run. Afterwards `systemctl --user list-units --all 'claude-aut6r14*'` listed 0 units, `~/.config/systemd/user` held 0 `aut6r14` links, and `breezy-quote-tape` and `breezy-nws-ingest` were still `active running`, untouched.
- **What was not touched.** No `breezy-*` unit, no operator cap, no enablement, no permit and no NO-SEND path.
- **Where the evidence is.** The sender and the journal extracts are in the session scratchpad `aut6r14/`.
